अब, P : K में y को प्रतिस्थापित करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ P : K = 3(8x38x38x38x38x38x3 ) : 2(8x38x38x38x38x38x3 )

\n\n

⇒ P : K = 8x : 16x316x316x316x316x316x3

\n\n

चूँकि J : P = 5x : 8x, हम संयुक्त अनुपात को निम्नानुसार लिख सकते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ J : P : K = 5x : 8x : 16x316x316x316x316x316x3

\n\n

सरलीकरण के लिए, प्रत्येक पद को 3 से गुणा करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ J : P : K = 15x : 24x : 16x

\n\n

अनुपातों का योग है:

\n\n

⇒ 15x + 24x + 16x = 55x

\n\n

दिया गया है कि कुल राशि ₹17,600 है:

\n\n

⇒ 55x = 17,600

\n\n

⇒ x = 17,6005517,6005517,6005517,6005517,6005517,60055

\n\n

⇒ x = 320

\n\n

अब, जीवन का हिस्सा ज्ञात करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ J = 15x

\n\n

⇒ J = 15 × 320

\n\n

⇒ J = 4,800

\n\n

∴ जीवन को अपने हिस्से के रूप में ₹4,800 प्राप्त हुए हैं।

\n\n

প্রবীণ এবং কানন দ্বারা প্রাপ্ত পরিমানের অনুপাত = 3:2

\n\n

ধারণা এবং ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

ব্যক্তিগত অংশগুলি খুঁজে পেতে, আমাদের জীবন, প্রবীণ এবং কাননের মিলিত অনুপাত নির্ধারণ করতে হবে।

\n\n

ধরা যাক, জীবন, প্রবীণ এবং কানন যথাক্রমে J, P এবং K টাকা পেয়েছে।

\n\n

প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

J : P = 5 : 8

\n\n

P : K = 3 : 2

\n\n

আমাদের J : P : K এর একটি সাধারণ অনুপাত খুঁজে বের করতে হবে।

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

আমরা পাই,

\n\n

⇒ J : P = 5 : 8 = 5x : 8x

\n\n

⇒ P : K = 3 : 2 = 3y : 2y

\n\n

যেহেতু P উভয় অনুপাতেই সাধারণ, তাই P এর দুটি প্রকাশকে সমান করুন:

\n\n

⇒ 8x = 3y

\n\n

⇒ y = 8x38x3

\n\n

এখন, P : K অনুপাতে y বসান:

\n\n

⇒ P : K = 3(8x38x3 ) : 2(8x38x3 )

\n\n

⇒ P : K = 8x : 16x316x3

\n\n

যেহেতু J : P = 5x : 8x, আমরা মিলিত অনুপাতটি লিখতে পারি:

\n\n

⇒ J : P : K = 5x : 8x : 16x316x3

\n\n

সরলীকরণের জন্য, প্রতিটি পদকে 3 দিয়ে গুণ করুন:

\n\n

⇒ J : P : K = 15x : 24x : 16x

\n\n

অনুপাতের যোগফল হল:

\n\n

⇒ 15x + 24x + 16x = 55x

\n\n

প্রদত্ত মোট অংক 17,600 টাকা:

\n\n

⇒ 55x = 17,600

\n\n

⇒ x = 17,6005517,60055

\n\n

⇒ x = 320

\n\n

এখন, জীবনের অংশ খুঁজে বের করুন:

\n\n

⇒ J = 15x

\n\n

⇒ J = 15 x 320

\n\n

⇒ J = 4,800

\n\n

∴ জীবন তার অংশ হিসেবে 4,800 টাকা পেয়েছে।

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

The money is divided such that:

\n\n

5 (Part of Ravi) = 3 (Part of Reeta) = 11 (Part of Rahul)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Let's assume:

\n\n

Part of Ravi = x

\n\n

Part of Reeta = y

\n\n

Part of Rahul = z

\n\n

We have:

\n\n

5x = 3y = 11z

\n\n

To find the ratio, express x, y, and z in terms of a common variable k:

\n\n

x = k/5

\n\n

y = k/3

\n\n

z = k/11

\n\n

The ratio Ravi : Reeta : Rahul is equal to x : y : z.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Find a common multiple for the denominators 5, 3, and 11, which is 165.

\n\n

So, multiply each part by 165:

\n\n

Ravi's share = (k/5) × 165 = 33k

\n\n

Reeta's share = (k/3) × 165 = 55k

\n\n

Rahul's share = (k/11) × 165 = 15k

\n\n

Therefore, the ratio Ravi : Reeta : Rahul is:

\n\n

∴ Ravi : Reeta : Rahul = 33 : 55 : 15

Given:

\n\n

A ∶ B = 3 ∶ 7 and B ∶ C = 2 ∶ 5

\n\n

Concept:

\n\n

To find the combined ratio A ∶ B ∶ C, we need to make the B values in both ratios equal.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

We have,

\n\n

A ∶ B = 3 ∶ 7

\n\n

B ∶ C = 2 ∶ 5

\n\n

First, find the common multiple of B in both ratios.

\n\n

⇒ LCM of 7 and 2 is 14

\n\n

Adjust the ratios to have the same B value:

\n\n

⇒ A ∶ B = 3 × 2 ∶ 7 × 2 = 6 ∶ 14

\n\n

⇒ B ∶ C = 2 × 7 ∶ 5 × 7 = 14 ∶ 35

\n\n

Now, combine the ratios:

\n\n

⇒ A ∶ B ∶ C = 6 ∶ 14 ∶ 35

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option 2 (6 ∶ 14 ∶ 35).

Given:

\n\n

The prices of a school bag and a school dress are in the ratio of 8:7. The price of the school bag is ₹400 more than the price of the school dress.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Let the price of the school dress be 7x and the price of the school bag be 8x.

\n\n

Price of the school bag = Price of the school dress + ₹400

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

8x = 7x + 400

\n\n

⇒ 8x - 7x = 400

\n\n

⇒ x = 400

\n\n

Price of the school dress = 7x = 7 × 400 = ₹2800

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (2).

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଏକ ସ୍କୁଲ ବ୍ୟାଗ୍ ଏବଂ ଏକ ସ୍କୁଲ ଡ୍ରେସର ମୂଲ୍ୟ 8:7 ଅନୁପାତରେ ଅଛି। ସ୍କୁଲ ବ୍ୟାଗର ମୂଲ୍ୟ ସ୍କୁଲ ଡ୍ରେସର ମୂଲ୍ୟଠାରୁ 400 ଟଙ୍କା ଅଧିକ।

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

ଧରନ୍ତୁ ସ୍କୁଲ ଡ୍ରେସର ମୂଲ୍ୟ 7x ଏବଂ ସ୍କୁଲ ବ୍ୟାଗର ମୂଲ୍ୟ 8x।

\n\n

ସ୍କୁଲ ବ୍ୟାଗର ମୂଲ୍ୟ = ସ୍କୁଲ ଡ୍ରେସର ମୂଲ୍ୟ + ₹400

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

8x = 7x + 400

\n\n

⇒ 8x - 7x = 400

\n\n

⇒ x = 400

\n\n

ସ୍କୁଲ ଡ୍ରେସର ମୂଲ୍ୟ = 7x = 7 x 400 = ₹2800

\n\n

∴ ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି ବିକଳ୍ପ (2)।

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

ಎರಡು ಸಂಖ್ಯೆಗಳು ಕ್ರಮವಾಗಿ, ಮೂರನೇ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆಗಿಂತ 30% ಮತ್ತು 60% ಹೆಚ್ಚು.

ಪರಿಕಲ್ಪನೆ:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

மூன்றாவது எண் x என்க.

\n\n

⇒ முதல் எண் = 1.30x

\n\n

இரண்டாவது எண் = x + x இன் 60%

\n\n

⇒ இரண்டாவது எண் = x + 0.60x

\n\n

⇒ இரண்டாவது எண் = 1.60x

\n\n

இரண்டு எண்களின் விகிதம் = முதல் எண் : இரண்டாவது எண்

\n\n

⇒ விகிதம் = 1.30x : 1.60x

\n\n

⇒ விகிதம் = 1.30 : 1.60

\n\n

⇒ விகிதம் = 13 : 16

\n\n

∴ சரியான விடை விருப்பம் 2, 13 : 16.

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଦୁଇଟି ସଂଖ୍ୟା ଯଥାକ୍ରମେ ତୃତୀୟ ସଂଖ୍ୟାଠାରୁ 30% ଏବଂ 60% ଅଧିକ।

Given:

\n\n

The ratio of the number of boys in a school to the total number of boys and girls in that school is 7 ∶ 17.

\n\n

The number of boys in that school is 1099,

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the number of boys be 7x,

\n\n

So the total number of  girls 17x - 7x = 10x

\n\n

As per the question,

\n\n

The value of 7x = 1099

\n\n

The value of 10x is 1099 × 10/7 = 1570

Given:

\n\n

If \\(\\dfrac{1}{M} : \\dfrac{1}{N} : \\dfrac{1}{O}\\) = 3 : 4 : 5

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

To find the ratio M : N : O , we use the property of inverse ratios.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{M}\\) = 3k

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{N}\\) = 4k

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{1}{O}\\) = 5k

\n\n

⇒ M = \\(\\dfrac{1}{3k}\\)

\n\n

⇒ N = \\(\\dfrac{1}{4k}\\)

\n\n

⇒ O = \\(\\dfrac{1}{5k}\\)

\n\n

To find the ratio M : N : O , we need to take the LCM of the denominators (3k, 4k, 5k), which is 60k.

\n\n

⇒ M = \\(\\dfrac{60k}{3k}\\) = 20

\n\n

⇒ N = \\(\\dfrac{60k}{4k}\\) = 15

\n\n

⇒ O = \\(\\dfrac{60k}{5k}\\) = 12

\n\n

∴ M : N : O = 20 : 15 : 12

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is option (2).

\n\n

Alternate Method   

\n\n

\\(\\frac{1}{M}: \\frac{1}{N}: \\frac{1}{O}=3: 4: 5\\)

\n\n

M : N : O = 1/3 : 1/4 : 1/5

\n\n

LCM (3,4,5) = 60 (Multiply by this every ratio)

\n\n

⇒ 20 : 15 : 12

The given ratio of the two numbers is 5 : 3.

\n

\n

We are given that 2 parts = 34 ⇒ 1 part = 17.

\n

The smaller number corresponds to 3 parts.

\n

⇒ Smaller number = 3 × 17 = 51.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 51.

Shortcut Trick\n

Difference in ratio parts of Q and R = 9 − 2 = 7 parts.

\n

Given 7 parts = ₹560 ⇒ 1 part = ₹80.

\n

Share of S = 3 parts.

\n

Share of S = 3 × 80 = ₹240.

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹240.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Ratio of shares of P, Q, R, and S = 4 : 9 : 2 : 3

\n

Difference between the shares of Q and R = ₹560

\n\n\n\n

Calculation:

\n

Let the shares of P, Q, R, and S be 4x, 9x, 2x, and 3x respectively.

\n

⇒ Share of Q − Share of R = 560

\n

⇒ 9x − 2x = 560

\n

⇒ 7x = 560

\n

⇒ x = 560 ÷ 7

\n

⇒ x = 80

\n

⇒ Share of S = 3x

\n

⇒ Share of S = 3 × 80 = 240

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹240.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Ratio & Proportion

\n

A ratio establishes a quantitative relation between two amounts, reflecting the number of times one contains the other. If quantities are in the ratio a : b, their actual values can be considered as ax and bx for calculation purposes.

\n\n

Unitary Method

\n

A foundational mathematical technique where the value of a single unit is determined first (e.g., 1 part = Total Difference ÷ Ratio Difference), and then multiplied by the required number of units to find the target answer.

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

The number of books on Mathematics, Physics and Chemistry in a University library is in the ratio 8 ∶ 5 ∶ 9.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the number of books be 8a, 5a and 9a

\n\n

New ratio = 8a × 110% : 5a × 105% : 9a × 105%

\n\n

⇒ 8a × 22 : 5a × 21 : 9a × 21

\n\n

⇒ 176 : 105 : 189

\n\n

चार संख्याएँ 1 ∶ 2 ∶ 3 ∶ প্রদত্ত:

\n\n

চারটি সংখ্যা 1 ∶ 2 ∶ 3 ∶ 5 অনুপাতে আছে 

\n\n

তাদের যোগফলের দ্বিগুন হল 44

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরি, সাধারণ গুণিতক x 

\n\n

প্রশ্ন অনুযায়ী,

\n\n

⇒ 2(1x + 2x + 3x + 5x) = 44

\n\n

⇒ 11x = \\(\\frac{44}{2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ x = \\(\\frac{22}{11}\\) = 2

\n\n

সুতরাং অনুপাত  = 2 : 4 : 6 : 10

\n\n

নতুন অনুপাত = (2 : 4 : 6 : 10)3 = 8 : 64 : 216 : 1000

\n\n

প্রতিটি পদ থেকে 3​ বিয়োগ করে পাই = 5 : 61 : 213 : 997

\n\n

∴ প্রতিটি সংখ্যার ঘনক থেকে 3 বিয়োগ করলে প্রাপ্ত সংখ্যার অনুপাত হল 5 : 61 : 213 : 997

\n55:T82b,\n

 Salary increases in the ratio 8 : 11

New salary is Rs. 33000

\n\n

Let the original salary be S

\n\n

According to the question

\n\n

⇒ 8 : 11 = S : 33000

\n\n

⇒ S =  24000 rupees

\n\n

∴ The correct choice will be option 4.

\n\n

Shortcut TrickConcept used:

\n\n

The number represents its ratio will be divisible by that ratio.

\n\n

11 = 33000

\n\n

1 = 3000

\n\n

8 = 24000

\n\n

Hence, the original salary is Rs. 24000.

Given

\n\n

Ratio of two numbers = 10 : 13

\n\n

80% of the first number = 16

\n\n

Concept Used

\n\n

Ratio Proportions

\n\n

Solution

\n\n

First number = 16/0.80 = 20

\n\n

Second number = 20 × 13/10 = 26

\n\n

50% of the second number = 26 × 0.50 = 13

\n\n

Therefore, 50% of the second number is 13

Given:

\n\n

Initial ratio = 5 : 11

\n\n

Final ratio = 3 : 5

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

If each term of the ratio a : b is increased by x, the new ratio becomes (a + x) : (b + x)

\n\n

Calculations:

\n\n

Let the number to be added to each term be x.

\n\n

(5 + x)/(11 + x) = 3/5

\n\n

⇒ 5(5 + x) = 3(11 + x)

\n\n

⇒ 25 + 5x = 33 + 3x

\n\n

⇒ 5x - 3x = 33 - 25

\n\n

⇒ 2x = 8

\n\n

⇒ x = 4

\n\n

∴ The correct number to be added to each term is 4.

\n", "hn": "

\n\n

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

\n\n

गणनाएँ:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि प्रत्येक पद में जोड़ा जाने वाला संख्या x है।

\n\n

(5 + x)/(11 + x) = 3/5

\n\n

⇒ 5(5 + x) = 3(11 + x)

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 5x - 3x = 33 - 25

\n\n

⇒ 2x = 8

\n\n

⇒ x = 4

\n\n

Given : 

\n\n

Salaries of a father, mother and daughter are in the ratio 2 ∶ 3 ∶ 5. 

\n\n

Calculation : 

\n\n

Let the Salaries of father, mother, daughter is 200, 300, 500.

\n\n

Increased salary of father = 200 × 115/100 = 230

\n\n

Increased salary of mother = 300 × 110/100 = 330

\n\n

Increased salary of daughter = 500 × 120/100 = 600

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Total ratio parts = 2 + 1 + 3 = 6 parts.

\n

Value of 1 part = 1200 ÷ 6 = ₹200.

\n

Required expression = 3 × (Largest Ratio Part) + 2 × (Smallest Ratio Part).

\n

Required parts = 3 × 3 + 2 × 1 = 9 + 2 = 11 parts.

\n

Required amount = 11 × 200 = ₹2,200.

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹2,200.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total amount to be divided = ₹1,200

\n

Ratio of shares among three friends = 2 : 1 : 3

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Sum of all ratio parts = 2 + 1 + 3 = 6 parts

\n

⇒ Value of 6 parts = ₹1,200

\n

⇒ Value of 1 part = 1200 ÷ 6 = ₹200

\n

⇒ Smallest share (1 part) = ₹200

\n

⇒ Largest share (3 parts) = 3 × 200 = ₹600

\n

⇒ Amount to find = 3 × Largest share + 2 × Smallest share

\n

⇒ Required amount = 3 × 600 + 2 × 200

\n

⇒ Required amount = 1800 + 400 = ₹2,200

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹2,200.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Proportions

\n

If a : b = c : d, then a, b, c, and d are in proportion. The fundamental rule states that the product of extremes equals the product of means (a × d = b × c).

\n

Mean Proportion

\n

The mean proportion between any two numbers a and b is given by the formula √(a × b).

\n

Compound Ratio

\n

For two given ratios a : b and c : d, their corresponding compound ratio is derived by multiplying the antecedents and consequents respectively, resulting in (a × c) : (b × d).

"ਇੱਕ ਕਲੱਬ ਵਿੱਚ ਮਰਦ ਅਤੇ ਔਰਤ ਮੈਂਬਰਾਂ ਦਾ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ 2 : 3 ਹੈ। ਜੇਕਰ ਮਰਦ ਮੈਂਬਰਾਂ ਦੀ ਗਿਣਤੀ 200 ਵਧ ਜਾਂਦੀ ਹੈ ਤਾਂ ਅਨੁਪਾਤ 5 : 6 ਹੋ ਜਾਂਦਾ ਹੈ। ਕਲੱਬ ਵਿੱਚ ਕਿੰਨੀਆਂ ਔਰਤ ਮੈਂਬਰ ਹਨ?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "1200"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "900"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "1400"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "1000"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Since the number of female members remains unchanged, we make the female parts equal in both ratios.

\n

Initial Ratio (M : F) = 2 : 3 = 4 : 6

\n

Final Ratio (M : F) = 5 : 6

\n

Increase in male parts = 5 − 4 = 1 part.

\n

Given, 1 part = 200, so female members = 6 parts = 6 × 200 = 1200.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1200.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Initial Ratio of Male to Female = 2 : 3

\n

Let the number of male members be 2x and female members be 3x.

\n

Increase in male members = 200

\n

New Ratio of Male to Female = 5 : 6

\n\n

Calculation:

\n

⇒ (2x + 200) / 3x = 5 / 6

\n

⇒ 6 × (2x + 200) = 5 × (3x)

\n

⇒ 12x + 1200 = 15x

\n

⇒ 15x − 12x = 1200

\n

⇒ 3x = 1200

\n

⇒ x = 400

\n

⇒ Number of female members = 3x = 3 × 400 = 1200

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1200.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Proportion

\n

When two ratios are equal, they are said to be in proportion. If a:b = c:d, then the product of extremes equals the product of means (ad = bc).

\n

Compound Ratio

\n

The compound ratio of two or more ratios is formed by multiplying their respective antecedents and consequents. For a:b and c:d, the compound ratio is ac:bd.

\n

Duplicate & Sub-duplicate Ratio

\n

The duplicate ratio of a:b is a2 : b2. The sub-duplicate ratio is √a : √b.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

किसी क्लब में पुरुष और महिला सदस्यों का अनुपात 2 : 3 है।

\n\n

यदि पुरुष सदस्यों की संख्या में 200 की वृद्धि की जाती है, तो अनुपात 5 : 6 हो जाता है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

मान लीजिए पुरुष सदस्यों की संख्या 2x और महिला सदस्यों की संख्या 3x है।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रारंभ में,

\n\n

पुरुष सदस्यों की संख्या = 2x

\n\n

महिला सदस्यों की संख्या = 3x

\n\n

वृद्धि के बाद,

\n\n

पुरुष सदस्यों की संख्या = 2x + 200

\n\n

नया अनुपात = 5 : 6

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2x + 200}{3x} = \\frac{5}{6}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 6(2x + 200) = 5(3x)

\n\n

⇒ 12x + 1200 = 15x

\n\n

⇒ 15x - 12x = 1200

\n\n

⇒ 3x = 1200

\n\n

⇒ x = 400

\n\n

महिला सदस्यों की संख्या = 3x = 3 × 400 = 1200

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर विकल्प 1 है।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ಹೆಚ್ಚಳದ ನಂತರ,

\n\n

ಗಂಡು ಸದಸ್ಯರ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆ = 2x + 200

\n\n

ಹೊಸ ಅನುಪಾತ = 5 : 6

\n\n

\n\n

∴ ಸರಿಯಾದ ಉತ್ತರ ಆಯ್ಕೆ 1.

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

\n\n

ஒரு சங்கத்தில் உள்ள ஆண்கள் மற்றும் பெண்கள் உறுப்பினர்களின் விகிதம் 2 : 3.

\n\n

ஆண்களின் எண்ணிக்கை 200 அதிகரித்தால், விகிதம் 5 : 6 ஆகிறது.

\n\n

பயன்படுத்தப்படும் சூத்திரம்:

\n\n

ஆண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை 2x ஆகவும், பெண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை 3x ஆகவும் இருக்கட்டும்.

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

ஆரம்பத்தில்,

\n\n

ஆண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை = 2x

\n\n

பெண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை = 3x

\n\n

அதிகரித்த பிறகு,

\n\n

ஆண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை = 2x + 200

\n\n

புதிய விகிதம் = 5 : 6

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2x + 200}{3x} = \\frac{5}{6}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 6(2x + 200) = 5(3x)

\n\n

⇒ 12x + 1200 = 15x

\n\n

⇒ 15x - 12x = 1200

\n\n

⇒ 3x = 1200

\n\n

⇒ x = 400

\n\n

பெண் உறுப்பினர்களின் எண்ணிக்கை = 3x = 3 x 400 = 1200

\n\n

∴ சரியான பதில் விருப்பம் 1.

"range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Let the initial ratio of seats be 6 : 4 : 7.

\n

To find the new ratio, multiply each by its respective growth percentage factor.

\n

New ratio = (6 × 120%) : (4 × 140%) : (7 × 160%)

\n

New ratio = 7.2 : 5.6 : 11.2

\n

Multiply by 10 and divide by 8 to get the simplest form ⇒ 9 : 7 : 14

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9 : 7 : 14.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

The initial ratio of seats for Mathematics, English, and Chemistry = 6 : 4 : 7

\n

Increase in seats = 20%, 40%, and 60% respectively.

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

Let the initial number of seats be 600, 400, and 700 to make percentage calculations easier.

\n

⇒ Increased seats in Mathematics = 600 + (20/100 × 600) = 600 + 120 = 720

\n

⇒ Increased seats in English = 400 + (40/100 × 400) = 400 + 160 = 560

\n

⇒ Increased seats in Chemistry = 700 + (60/100 × 700) = 700 + 420 = 1120

\n

⇒ New ratio of seats = 720 : 560 : 1120

\n

⇒ Divide all terms by 80 to get the simplified equivalent ratio.

\n

⇒ New ratio = (720 ÷ 80) : (560 ÷ 80) : (1120 ÷ 80)

\n

⇒ New ratio = 9 : 7 : 14

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9 : 7 : 14.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Compound Ratio

\n

When two or more ratios are multiplied term by term, the resulting ratio is called a compound ratio. For a:b and c:d, the compound ratio is ac:bd.

\n

Percentage Increase

\n

When a value increases by R%, the new value is directly calculated as Initial Value × (100 + R)/100.

\n

Scaling in Ratios

\n

Multiplying or dividing all terms of a ratio by the same non-zero number does not change the proportion. This property is frequently used to clear decimals or fractions.

\n\n

सीटों में 20%, 40% और 60% की वृद्धि की गई है।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना कि गणित, अंग्रेजी और रसायन विज्ञान की सीटें 600, 400, 700 हैं।

\n\n

सीटों में 20%, 40% और 60% की वृद्धि की गई है।

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 600 × 120 : 400 × 140 : 700 × 160

\n\n

⇒ 72000 : 56000 : 112000

\n\n

⇒ 72 : 56 : 112

\n\n

⇒ 9 : 7 : 14

\n\n

∴ सही उत्तर 9 : 7 : 14 है।

\n\n

ఒక పాఠశాలలో గణితం, ఇంగ్లిష్ మరియు కెమిస్ట్రీ కొరకు సీట్లు 6 ∶ 4 ∶ 7 నిష్పత్తిలో  ఉంటాయి.

\n\n

\n\n

Given:

\n\n

Two numbers are in the ratio 4:5.

\n\n

If 3 is added to the smaller and 7 is subtracted from the larger number, they are in the ratio 1:1.

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n\n

We will use the concept of ratios and algebra to solve the problem.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the smaller number be 4x and the larger number be 5x.

\n\n

Given, (4x + 3) / (5x - 7) = 1 / 1

\n\n

⇒ 4x + 3 = 5x - 7

\n\n

⇒ 4x - 5x = -7 - 3

\n\n

⇒ -x = -10

\n\n

⇒ x = 10

\n\n

The numbers are:

\n\n

⇒ Smaller number = 4x = 4 × 10 = 40

\n\n

⇒ Larger number = 5x = 5 × 10 = 50

\n\n

∴ The numbers are 40 and 50.

\n\n

हम इस प्रश्न को हल करने के लिए अनुपात और बीजगणित की अवधारणा का उपयोग करेंगे।

Shortcut Trick\n

Directly multiply the given ratio terms by their respective percentage multipliers after the increase.

New Ratio = (3 × 110) : (4 × 115)

\n

New Ratio = 330 : 460 = 33 : 46

\n

∴ The correct answer is 33/46.

\n\n

"end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "

Given:

\n\n

Two numbers are in the ratio 4:5. If 17 is subtracted from each, the new numbers are in the ratio 11:14.

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

Let the two numbers be 4x and 5x.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

After subtracting 17 from each, the new numbers become (4x - 17) and (5x - 17).दिया गया है:

\n\n

दो संख्याएँ 4:5 के अनुपात में हैं। यदि प्रत्येक संख्या से 17 घटाया जाता है, तो नई संख्याएँ 11:14 के अनुपात में हो जाती हैं।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि दो संख्याएँ 4x और 5x हैं।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

प्रत्येक से 17 घटाने के बाद, नई संख्याएँ (4x - 17) और (5x - 17) बन जाती हैं।

\n\n

दिया गया नया अनुपात: (4x - 17) / (5x - 17) = 11 / 14

\n\n

⇒ 14(4x - 17) = 11(5x - 17)

\n\n

⇒ 56x - 238 = 55x - 187

\n\n

⇒ 56x - 55x = 238 - 187

\n\n

⇒ x = 51

\n\n

मूल संख्याएँ 4x = 4×51 = 204 और 5x = 5 × 51 = 255 हैं।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

नया अनुपात: 224 : 275

Given:

\n\n

A sum of money is divided among A, B, and C in the ratio 3 ∶ 2 ∶ 5

\n\n

If the share of C is Rs. 300 more than B.

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Let the share of money with A, B, and C be 3x, 2x and 5x.

\n\n

The difference between the share of B and C = 5x - 2x = 3x

\n\n

⇒ 3x = 300

\n\n

⇒ x = 100

\n\n

The difference between the share of B and A = 3x - 2x = x

\n\n

Since, x = 100, A gets 100 more than B.

\n\n

The answer is 100.

\n\n

\n\n
